華潤集團於中國的城市燃氣                       THE PRC CITY GAS DISTRIBUTION FLAGSHIP
分銷旗艦                               OF CHINA RESOURCES GROUP
華潤燃氣控股有限公司（「華潤燃氣」）主要               China Resources Gas Group Limited (“CR Gas”), substantially owned
由中國最大的國有企業集團之一的華潤（集                by China Resources (Holdings) Company Limited, one of the largest
團）有限公司擁有。作為中國領先的燃氣公                state-owned conglomerates in China, is a leading gas utilities group in
用事業集團，華潤燃氣主要從事下游城市燃                China, which is principally engaged in downstream city gas distribution
氣分銷業務，包括管道天然氣分銷及天然氣                business including piped natural gas distribution, natural gas filling
加氣站業務及燃氣器具銷售。華潤燃氣的業                stations operation and sales of gas appliances. Its operations are
務策略性地分佈於全國各地，主要位於經濟                strategically located in areas of China, which are economically more
較發達和人口密集的地區以及天然氣儲量豐                developed and densely populated and areas with rich reserves of
富的地區。                              natural gas.

憑藉良好的行業基礎及本集團的執行能                  Leveraging on the excellent industry fundamentals and the Group’s
力，華潤燃氣於二零一九年繼續通過內涵                 execution competency, CR Gas continues to expand rapidly in 2019 via
式增長及收購實現快速擴張。於二零一九                 organic and acquisition growths. At the end of 2019, CR Gas’s portfolio is
年末，華潤燃氣的城市燃氣項目總共達251               consisted of 251 city gas projects in 22 provinces of China including 14
個，分佈於中國22個省份，其中包括14個               provincial capitals and 3 direct administrative municipalities with an annual
省會城巿及3個直轄市，年燃氣總銷量達約                gross gas sales volume of some 28.0 billion cubic meters and 37.95 million
280億立方米及客戶數達3,795萬戶。               customers.

能源結構優化和環境污染治理將推動中國天                The optimization of energy structure and environmental pollution control will
然氣消費持續增長。中國政府近年來採取                 promote the sustainable growth of natural gas consumption in China. The
多項措施提倡對污染較少的能源資源的發展                PRC government has, in recent years, taken various measures to promote
及使用。同煤炭及原油等傳統能源資源相                 the development and utilization of less polluting energy sources. The
比， 天 然 氣 產 業 符 合 國 家 創 新、 協 調、 綠   natural gas industry is in sync with the core development idea of innovation,
色、開放、共享的核心發展理念，國家出台                harmony, green, open and sharing as compared with conventional energy
的《天 然 氣 發 展「十 三 五」規 劃》也 再 次 強      resources such as coal and crude oil. The Natural Gas Development “13th
調天然氣廣泛使用對保護生態環境，改善大                Five-Year” Plan published by the State also stresses that the popular use of
氣質量，提高公眾生活質量和健康水平，實                natural gas will play an important role to protect the ecological environment,
現可持續發展具有重要作用。                      improve air quality, enhance life quality and health of the general public
                                   and achieve sustainable development.

中國仍是全球天然氣發展最重要區域，中國                The natural gas market in China is still in the important development
天然氣市仍處於重要發展期，華潤燃氣將會                period, as China remains the most important region in the global rise
充分利用有利經營環境，透過內涵及外延式                of natural gas development. CR Gas is well placed to take advantage
增長持續擴張、在追求卓越表現的同時努力                of such a favorable operating environment and to become the most
提升其經營效率、提供安全可靠的清潔能源                respectable leader in the PRC’s gas industry by continuously expanding
供應並向客戶提供體貼、周到及高效的服                 through organic and external growths; diligently enhancing its operational
務，向股東提供不斷增長且可持續的業績表                efficiency in pursue of excellence; delivering a safe and reliable supply of
現，為僱員創造廣泛且專業的個人發展機                 clean energy as well as the caring, competent and efficient service to our
會，為中國及世界的環境保護及改善做出貢                customers; offering growing and sustainable performance to shareholders;
獻，成為中國燃氣行業最受尊敬的領導者。                creating extensive professional and personal development opportunities
                                   to employees; and contributing to the protection and improvement of the
                                   Chinese and global environment.

本集團將透過持續性的內涵增長及高質量外延式擴張，繼續穩健的發
展，同時積極探討燃氣價值鏈及客戶所能帶來的業務商機。

The Group will expand steadily via sustainable organic growth and
quality external acquisitions, at the same time actively explore new
opportunities arising from its gas value chain and customer base.

深挖工商業市場潛力，積極推動工商業用                 of the opportunity to prevent and combat air pollution, the Group tapped
戶使用天然氣，實現銷氣量快速增長。年                 on the potential of the industrial and commercial market and actively

     精準學標桿                                      PRECISE BENCHMARKING
     「學標桿」是一個將自身業務流程及績效指                        “Benchmarking” is the process of comparing one’s business processes
     標與業內最佳實踐者進行比較的過程。本集                        and performance metrics to the best practices of the industry. The
     團已著手在所有業務部門推行向最佳實踐者                        Group has embarked on this best practice to benchmark across all
     學標桿舉措。「學標桿」已開始在集團內成                        business units. “Benchmarking” has started off internally with intra-group
     員公司間進行，並已拓展至與行業外部最佳                        benchmarking and have extended to benchmarking against external
     實踐者對標。                                     best practices of the industry.


     推行「學標桿」是華潤燃氣實現增長方式由                        Implementing “Benchmarking” measure is an indispensable choice for
     資源投入向效率提升這一根本轉變的必然選                        CR Gas to accomplish the fundamental transformation of its growth
     擇，也是華潤燃氣確保達成戰略目標，成為                        model from resources-driven growth to efficiency improvement, and is
     「中國第一、世界一流」燃氣企業的重要舉                        also one crucial approach taken by CR Gas to achieve its strategic goal
     措。                                         to become the leading world-class gas enterprise in China.


     內部及外部學標桿活動相結合將有助於各成                        This combination of internal and external benchmarking helps each
     員公司識別其現階段表現與其預期之間的最                        member company to identify the most important gaps between its
     重要差距，從而就作出改變而制訂詳細計劃                        current state and where it wants to be. It creates a road map for change
     及嚴格的必要成本控制及表現基準，以在削                        and generates the rigorous cost and performance baseline necessary
     減成本及提升表現的同時有效追蹤該詳細計                        to effectively track progress on that road map over time in terms of cost
     劃的實施進度。所有成員公司不僅須與總部                        reductions and performance improvements. All member companies
     簽署「學標桿責任合同」，而且從過去38條                       were required to sign “Benchmarking Responsibility Contracts” with
     學標桿標準優化成28項量化指標突出管理                        head office, and the 38 benchmarks previously identified were further
     重點，使對標更加精準。該持續學標桿活動                        streamlined into 28 quantitative benchmarks, highlighting the key
     於二零一九年取得滿意的成績。                             objectives and becoming more focused and precise. This ongoing
                                                campaign has led to many satisfying results in 2019.

     董事會成員多元化政策                                 BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY
     本公司於二零一三年八月二十三日採納董                         The Company adopted a board diversity policy (the “Policy”) on 23rd
     事會成員多元化政策（「政策」）。此政策之                       August, 2013. A summary of this Policy, together with the measurable
     概要連同為執行此政策而制定之可計量目                         objectives set for implementing this Policy, and the progress made
     標及達標進度於下文披露。                               towards achieving those objectives are disclosed as below.


     政策概要                                       Summary of the Policy

     本公司視董事會層面的多元化為維持競爭                         The Company sees diversity at Board level as an essential element in
     優勢的重要元素。一個真正多元化的董事                         maintaining a competitive advantage. A truly diverse Board will include
     會 將 包 括 具 備 不 同 技 能、 地 區 及 行 業 經           and make good use of differences in the skills, regional and industry
     驗、 背 景、 性 別 及 其 他 特 質 的 董 事 會 成            experience, background, gender and other qualities of the members
     員，並可加以利用。該等差異將於釐定董                         of the Board. These differences will be considered in determining
     事會的最適合組成時予以考慮，並於可能                         the optimum composition of the Board and when possible should be
     情況下保持適當平衡。董事會所有委任均                         balanced appropriately. All Board appointments are made on merit, in
     按董事會整體有效運作所需要的技能及經                         the content of the skills and experience the Board as a whole requires
     驗水平作出。                                     to be effective.

提名政策                                Nomination Policy

提名政策旨在確保本公司的董事會董事                   Nomination policy aims to ensure that the board of directors of the
（「董事會」）在技能、經驗、知識及多元化                Company (the “Board”) has a balance of skills, experience, knowledge
觀點方面取得平衡，切合本公司的業務要                  and diversity of perspectives appropriate to the requirements of the
求。                                  Company’s business.


提名委員會將會妥為考慮以下條件（統稱為                 The Nomination Committee will evaluate, select and recommend
「該等條件」）以評核、甄選及向董事會建議                candidate(s) for directorships to the Board by giving due consideration
一名或多名候選人擔任董事，該等條件包                  to criteria including but not limited to (collectively, the “Criteria”):
括但不限於：


(a)   多元化觀點，包括但不限於性別、年              (a)   Diversity in aspects including but not limited to gender, age,
      齡、 文 化 背 景 及 教 育 背 景、 專 業 經         cultural and educational background, professional experience,
      驗、技能、知識及服務年期；                       skills, knowledge and length of service;

風險管理及內部監控                     RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL
董事會整體負責維持有效的風險管理及內            The Board has the overall responsibility of maintaining an effective risk
部監控系統，為重大的失實陳述或損失作            management and internal control system to provide reasonable, though not
出合理而非絕對的保證，並管理而非消除            absolute, assurance against material misstatement or loss and to manage
未能達到業務目標的風險。                  rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business objectives.


董事會透過審核與風險管理委員會，對本            The Board, through the Audit and Risk Management Committee, conducts
公司風險管理及內部監控系統對應的所有            annual review of the effectiveness of the Company’s systems of risk
重大監控的有效性進行年度檢討，包括本            management and internal control covering all material controls, including
公司的財務監控、內部監控及風險管理系            financial controls, internal control and risk management systems.
統。


審核與風險管理委員會應董事會的委派或            The Audit and Risk Management Committee considers the major
主動就有關風險管理及內部監控事宜的重            investigation findings on risk management and internal control matter
要調查結果及管理層對調查結果的回應進            as delegated by the Board or on its own initiative and management’s
行研究。                          response to these findings.


審核與風險管理委員會亦考慮本公司會             The Audit and Risk Management Committee also considers the
計、財務申報和內部審核職能的資源、資            adequacy of resources, qualifications and experience of the Company’s
格和經驗及彼等的培訓計劃和預算是否充            accounting, financial reporting and internal audit functions, and their
足。就處理及發佈內幕消息的程序及內部            training programmes and budget. With respect to procedures and
監控而言，本公司已制定有關處理內幕消            internal controls for handling and dissemination of inside information,
息的書面政策及程序，包括但不限於確保            the Company has set out written policies and procedures in relation to
符合披露要求的措施。                    the handling of inside information, including but not limited to measures
                              to ensure compliance of disclosure requirement.


內部審核部協助審核與風險管理委員會檢            The Internal Audit Division assists the Audit and Risk Management
討現行風險管理及內部監控系統運作的效            Committee to review the effectiveness of such risk management and
能。該部門定期進行內部審核及其他相關            internal control system in operation. The Division regularly carries out
的審計審核，並向審核與風險管理委員會            internal audit and other related audit reviews and reports its findings
報告所得結果及提供內部監控的改良建             and suggestions for improvement on better internal controls to the
議，以供審核與風險管理委員會考慮。年            Audit and Risk Management Committee for their consideration. During
內，內部審核部亦曾對本公司主要風險管            the year, the Internal Audit Division also conducted reviews of key risk
理系統進行檢討，並向審核與風險管理委            management system of the Company. Its findings were reported to the
員會報告所得結果以供考慮，且作為日後            Audit and Risk Management Committee for consideration and would
進一步改進的基礎。在經營環境（特許權的           form the basis for further improvement in the future. Inspections and
有效性及其保護、管道基礎設施瓶頸、策            risk assessments were carried out in key risk areas such as operation
略執行障礙等）；財務管理（現金保管及管           environment (concession rights availability and their protection, pipeline
理、付款審批程 序、 應 收 款 管 理、 會 計 政   infrastructure bottlenecks, strategy execution hurdles, etc); financial
策遵守情況等）；安全管理（管道基礎設施           management (cash custody and management, payment approval
檢查及維護程序、安全體系及相關安全事            process, receivables management, accounting policy adherence, etc);

股東權利                    SHAREHOLDERS’ RIGHTS
股東召開股東特別大會的程序           Procedures for shareholders to convene special general meeting
根據百慕達一九八一年公司法（「公司法」）    In accordance with the Section 74 of the Companies Act 1981 of
第74條，於遞呈要求日期持有本公司不少     Bermuda (“Companies Act”), the shareholders of the Company holding
於（賦於本公司股東大會上之投票權）十分     at the date of the deposit of the requisition not less than one-tenth of
之一繳足股本的本公司股東有權召開本公司     such of the paid-up capital of the Company carrying the right of voting
股東特別大會。倘董事於上述要求之送達日     at general meetings of the Company, shall have the right to convene a
期二十一日內未有召開會議，代表總投票權     special general meeting of the Company. If the directors do not within
半數以上的呈請人可自行召開會議。        twenty-one days from the date of the deposit of the requisition proceed
                        duly to convene a meeting, the requisitionists representing more than
                        one half of the total voting rights may themselves convene a meeting.

召開股東特別大會的書面要求可提交至本公     The written requisition for the special general meeting can be lodged at
司的香港主要辦事處，地址為香港灣仔港灣     the Company’s principal office in Hong Kong at Room 1901-02, China
道26號華潤大廈1901-02室。       Resources Building, 26 Harbour Road, Wanchai, Hong Kong.

本不少於二十分之一(5%)之登記股東（「呈   one-twentieth (5%) of the paid-up capital of the Company carrying the
請人」），或不少於100名有關登記股東，可   right of voting at general meetings of the Company (“Requisitionists”),

任何可能於該大會上正式動議及擬於會上動     notice of the next general meeting notice of any resolution which may

     長期獎勵計劃                                     LONG TERM AWARD SCHEME
     於二零零八年十二月一日，本公司採納一項                        On 1st December, 2008, the Company adopted a share repurchased
     股份購回現金獎勵計劃（「獎勵計劃」），據                       cash award scheme (the “Incentive Award Scheme”), pursuant to which
     此，本集團或投資公司的經甄選僱員皆有權                        selected employees of the Group or investee companies (being any
     根據計劃規則參加獎勵計劃。投資公司乃指                        company in which the Company directly or indirectly owns 5% or more
     任何一家由本公司直接或間接擁有5%或以                        attributable equity interests (the “Investee Company”), and in calculating
     上歸屬權益的公司（「投資公司」），就計算                       the percentage interest so attributed, the percentage interest held in
     歸屬權益百分比而言，在投資公司所持有權                        the Investee Company shall be multiplied by the percentage interest in
     益的百分比須乘以本集團透過其他公司持有                        each level of entity through which such interest is held by the Group),
     該權益的該等中間公司的百分比。                            are entitled to participate in the Incentive Award Scheme pursuant to
                                                the scheme rules.


     獎勵計劃旨在嘉許若干僱員的貢獻及鼓勵彼                        The purpose of the Incentive Award Scheme is to recognise and
     等作出貢獻，並提供獎勵及協助本集團及投                        motivate the contribution of certain employees and to provide incentives
     資公司挽留其現有僱員及延聘更多僱員，並                        and to enable the Group and the Investee Company in retaining their
     就達到本公司的長期商業目標向彼等提供直                        existing employees and recruiting additional employees and to provide
     接經濟利益。除非被董事會提前終止，獎勵                        them with a direct economic interest in attaining the long-term business
     計劃自採納日期起十年內有效。根據獎勵計                        objectives of the Company. Unless early termination by the Board, the
     劃授出獎勵股份及於其整個有效期內授予經                        Incentive Award Scheme shall be valid and effective for a term of 10
     甄選僱員獎勵股份的總數上限分別以本公司                        years commencing on the adoption date. The maximum aggregate
     不時已發行股本的5%及0.5%為限。                         numbers of award shares which can be awarded under the Incentive
                                                Award Scheme and to a selected employee throughout its duration are
                                                limited to 5% and 0.5% of the issued share capital of the Company from
                                                time to time respectively.

                                                statements

     我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否                         Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the
     不存在由於舞弊或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤                         consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from material
     陳述取得合理保證，並出具包括我們意見                         misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue an auditor’s

     法》第90條我們僅向全體成員報告。除此之                       a body, in accordance with Section 90 of the Bermuda Companies
     外，我們的報告不可用作其他用途。我們                         Act 1981, and for no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility
     並不就我們報告的內容對任何其他人士承                         towards or accept liability to any other person for the contents of this
     擔任何責任或接受任何義務。                              report.


     合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按                         Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a
     照香港審計準則進行的審計，在某一重大                         guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will
     錯誤陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以                         always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements can
     由舞弊或錯誤引起，如果合理預期它們單                         arise from fraud or error and are considered material if, individually or
     獨或匯總起來可能影響綜合財務報表使用                         in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected to influence the
     者依賴綜合財務報表所作出的經濟決定，                         economic decisions of users taken on the basis of these consolidated
     則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。                            financial statements.


     在根據香港審計準則進行審計的過程中，                         As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise professional
     我們運用了專業判斷，保持了職業懷疑態                         judgment and maintain professional scepticism throughout the audit.
     度。我們亦：                                     We also:

         風險，以及獲取充足和適當的審計憑                            and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and appropriate to
         證，作為我們意見的基礎。由於舞弊                            provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of not detecting a material
         可能涉及串謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛                            misstatement resulting from fraud is higher than for one resulting
         假陳述，或凌駕於內部控制之上，因                            from error, as fraud may involve collusion, forgery, intentional
         此未能發現因舞弊而導致的重大錯誤                            omissions, misrepresentations, or the override of internal control.
         陳述的風險高於未能發現因錯誤而導

  公司」），該公司為一家於中華人民共                        as “China Resources National Corp.”), a company established in
  和國（「中國」）成立的公司。本公司註                       the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”). The addresses of the

  年報內公司資料一節披露。                             are disclosed in the Corporate Information of the annual report.

4. 主要會計政策                        4. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
  綜合財務報表乃按照香港會計師公會                    The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in
  頒 佈 的 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 編 製。 此        accordance with HKFRSs issued by the HKICPA. In addition, the
  外，綜合財務報表亦包括聯交所證券                    consolidated financial statements include applicable disclosures
  上市規則及香港公司條例規定的適用                    required by the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the
  披露。                                 Stock Exchange and by the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.


  除於各個報告期末按公平值計算的若                    The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on the
  干金融工具外，綜合財務報表乃根據                    historical cost basis except for certain financial instruments which
  歷史成本基準編製，已在下列會計政                    are measured at fair values at the end of each reporting period, as
  策中作出解釋。                             explained in the accounting policies set out below.


  歷史成本一般以交換貨品及服務時給                    Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the
  予代價的公平值為基準。                         consideration given in exchange for goods and services.


  公平值是於計量日期市場參與者於有                    Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or
  秩序交易中出售資產可收取或轉讓負                    paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market
  債須支付的價格，而不論該價格是否                    participants at the measurement date, regardless of whether that
  直接可觀察或可使用其他估值技術估                    price is directly observable or estimated using another valuation
  計。若市場參與者於計量日期對資產                    technique. In estimating the fair value of an asset or a liability,
  或負債定價時會考慮資產或負債的特                    the Group takes into account the characteristics of the asset or
  點，則本集團於估計資產或負債的公                    liability if market participants would take those characteristics into
  平值時會考慮該等特點。此等綜合財                    account when pricing the asset or liability at the measurement
  務報表中作計量及 ╱ 或披露用途的公                  date. Fair value for measurement and/or disclosure purposes in
  平值乃按此基準釐定，惟屬於香港財                    these consolidated financial statements is determined on such a
  務報告準則第2號「以股份為基礎的付                   basis, except for share-based payment transactions that are within
  款」範圍的以股份付款的交易、屬於                    the scope of HKFRS 2 Share-based payment, leasing transactions
  香港會計準則第17號範圍內的租賃交                   that are within the scope of HKAS 17, and measurements that have
  易，以及與公平值有部分相若地方但                    some similarities to fair value but are not fair value, such as net
  並非公平值的計量，譬如香港會計準                    realisable value in HKAS 2 Inventories or value in use in HKAS 36
  則第2號「存貨」內的可變現淨額或香                   Impairment of assets.
  港 會 計 準 則 第36號「資 產 減 值」的 使
  用價值除外。


  此外，就財務呈報而言，公平值計量                    In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value
  根據公平值計量的輸入數據可觀察程                    measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on the
  度及公平值計量的輸入數據對其整體                    degree to which the inputs to the fair value measurements are
  的重要性分類為第一級、第二級或第                    observable and the significance of the inputs to the fair value
  三級，詳情如下：                            measurement in its entirety, which are described as follows:


  •   第一級輸入數據是實體於計量日                  •    Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active
      期可以取得的相同資產或負債於                       markets for identical assets or liabilities that the entity can
      活躍市場之報價（未經調整）；                       access at the measurement date;


  •   第二級輸入數據是就資產或負債                  •    Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices included
      直接或間接地可觀察之輸入數據                       within Level 1, that are observable for the asset or liability,
      （第一級內包括的報價除外）；及                      either directly or indirectly; and


  •   第三級輸入數據是資產或負債的                  •    Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or
      不可觀察輸入數據。                            liability.

                                                    (Continued)
          合併基準                                        Basis of consolidation

          綜合財務報表包括本公司及其所控制                            The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial
          實體及其附屬公司的財務報表。倘本                            statements of the Company and entities controlled by the Company
          公司符合以下條件時，即取得控制權：                           and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved where the Company:


          •   可對投資對象行使權力；                              •       has power over the investee;


          •   因參與投資對象業務而承受浮動                           •       is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its
              回報的風險或享有權利；及                                     involvement with the investee; and


          •   有能力動用其權力影響其回報。                           •       has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.


          倘有事實及情況顯示上述三項控制因                            The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if facts
          素之一項或多項出現變化，本集團將                            and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one or more
          重新評估其是否控制投資對象。                              of the three elements of control listed above.


          倘本集團於投資對象的投票權未能佔                            When the Group has less than a majority of the voting rights of
          大多數，但只要投票權足以賦予本集                            an investee, it has power over the investee when the voting rights
          團實際能力可單方面掌控投資對象之                            are sufficient to give it the practical ability to direct the relevant
          相關業務時，本集團即對投資對象擁                            activities of the investee unilaterally. The Group considers all
          有權力。在評估本集團於投資對象的                            relevant facts and circumstances in assessing whether or not the
          投票權是否足以賦予其權力時，本集                            Group’s voting rights in an investee are sufficient to give it power,
          團考慮所有相關事實及情況，包括：                            including:


          •   本集團持有投票權之規模相較其                           •       the size of the Group’s holding of voting rights relative to the
              他投票權持有人所持投票權之規                                   size and dispersion of holdings of the other vote holders;
              模及分散度；


          •   本集團、其他投票權持有人或其                           •       potential voting rights held by the Group, other vote holders
              他人士持有之潛在投票權；                                     or other parties;


          •   其他合同安排產生之權利；及                            •       rights arising from other contractual arrangements; and


          •   於需要作出決定時，本集團當前                           •       the current ability to direct the relevant activities at the time
              能否掌控相關活動的任何其他事                                   that decisions need to be made, including voting patterns at
              實及情況（包括於過往股東大會                                   previous shareholders’ meetings.
              上的投票方式）。

         購之可識別資產與所承擔負債於收購                             date amounts of the identifiable assets acquired and liabilities
         日期之淨額高於轉撥之代價、非控股                             assumed exceeds the sum of the consideration transferred, the
         權益於被收購方中所佔金額以及收購                             amount of any non-controlling interests in the acquiree and the
         方以往持有之被收購方股權之公平值                             fair value of the acquirer’s previously held interest in the acquiree
         （如有）之總和，則差額即時於損益內                            (if any), the excess is recognised immediately in profit or loss as
         確認為議價收購收益。                                   a bargain purchase gain.

          享有控制權的各方一致同意方能決定                            parties sharing control.

  終止確認。於終止確認無形資產時產         use of the asset. Any gain or loss arising from derecognition of

          金融資產減值                                      Impairment of financial assets

          本集團確認對並非按公平值計入損益                            The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit
          持有的所有債務工具預期信貸虧損                             losses(“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value through
         （「預期信貸虧損」）的撥備。預期信貸                           profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference between the
          虧損乃基於根據合同到期的合同現金                            contractual cash flows due in accordance with the contract and all
          流量與本集團預期收取的所有現金流                            the cash flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an
          量之間的差額而釐定，並以原實際利                            approximation of the original effective interest rate. The expected
          率的近似值貼現。預期現金流量將包                            cash flows will include cash flows from the sale of collateral held
          括出售所持抵押的現金流量或組成合                            or other credit enhancements that are integral to the contractual
          同條款的其他信貸提升措施。                               terms.


          一般方法                                        General approach

          預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自                            ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for
          初步確認起未有顯著增加的信貸風險                            which there has not been a significant increase in credit risk since
          而言，就未來12個月內可能發生的違                           initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses that result
          約事件而導致的信貸虧損（12個月預                           from default events that are possible within the next 12 months (a
          期信貸虧損）計提預期信貸虧損。就                            12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for which there has
          自初步確認起經已顯著增加的信貸風                            been a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,
          險而言，不論何時發生違約，於餘下                            a loss allowance is required for credit losses expected over the
          風險年期內的預期信貸虧損均須計提                            remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of the
          虧損撥備（全期預期信貸虧損）。                             default (a lifetime ECL).

          轉讓至本集團，則撇銷貸款及應收款                            together with any associated allowance are written off when there is
          項連同任何相關撥備。                                  no realistic prospect of future recovery and all collateral has been
                                                      realised or has been transferred to the Group.


          倘若在其後期間估計減值虧損金額由                            If, in a subsequent period, the amount of the estimated impairment
          於確認減值之後發生的事項增加或減                            loss increases or decreases because of an event occurring
          少，則透過調整撥備賬增加或減少先                            after the impairment was recognised, the previously recognised
          前確認的減值虧損。倘於其後收回撇                            impairment loss is increased or reduced by adjusting the allowance
          銷，該項收回將計入損益及其他全面                            account. If a write-off is later recovered, the recovery is credited
          收益表內的其他開支。                                  to other expenses in the statement of profit or loss and other
                                                      comprehensive income.


          以成本列值之資產                                    Assets carried at cost

          倘有客觀跡象顯示因其公平值不能可                            If there is objective evidence that an impairment loss has been
          靠計量而不以公平值列值之無報價權                            incurred on an unquoted equity instrument that is not carried at
          益工具或與須交付該項無報價權益工                            fair value because its fair value cannot be reliably measured, or on
          具相關並須通過該項交付結算的衍生                            a derivative asset that is linked to and must be settled by delivery
          資產出現減值虧損，虧損金額會按資                            of such an unquoted equity instrument, the amount of the loss is
          產賬面值與以類似金融資產當時之市                            measured as the difference between the asset’s carrying amount
          場回報率對估計未來現金流量折現後                            and the present value of estimated future cash flows discounted
          之現值兩者間之差額計量。該等資產                            at the current market rate of return for a similar financial asset.
          之減值虧損不會撥回。                                  Impairment losses on these assets are not reversed.

          算。應課稅溢利不包括其他年度的應                            Taxable profit differs from profit before taxation as reported in the

          須課稅或不可扣稅項目，因而與綜合                            income because it excludes items of income or expense that are

          負債乃按在報告期末已頒佈或實質頒                            current tax is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or
          佈的稅率計算。                                     substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.

          確認入賬。遞延稅項資產通常於可能                            recognised for all taxable temporary differences. Deferred tax
          有應課稅溢利可用於抵銷所有可扣稅                            assets are generally recognised for all deductible temporary
          暫時差額時就該等可扣稅暫時差額確                            differences to the extent that it is probable that taxable profit will

          項不會對應課稅溢利及會計溢利造成                            can be utilised. Such assets and liabilities are not recognised if
          影響的交易中首次確認（業務合併除                            the temporary difference arises from goodwill or from the initial

          暫時差額確認，惟倘本集團可控制暫                            and joint ventures, except where the Group is able to control the

          非貨幣項目，按釐定公平值日期的適                            Non-monetary items carried at fair value that are denominated in

          量的非貨幣項目則不會重新換算。                             date when the fair value was determined. Non-monetary items that

          結算貨幣項目及重新換算貨幣項目所                            Exchange differences arising on the settlement of monetary items,
          產生之匯兌差額，於其產生期間在損                            and on the retranslation of monetary items, are recognised in profit

          貨幣項目的匯兌差額除外，就此，有                            differences on monetary items receivable from or payable to a

          涉及未來的主要假設及於報告期間末                            The followings are the key assumptions concerning the future,

          物業、廠房及設備的可使用年期估計                            Estimation of useful lives of property, plant and equipment

          管理層根據物業、廠房及設備的預期                            Management estimates the useful lives of property, plant and
          壽命估計其可使用年期。物業、廠房                            equipment based on the expected lifespan of those property, plant
          及設備的可使用年期可能因技術創新                            and equipment. The useful lives of property, plant and equipment
          而出現重大變動。物業、廠房及設備                            could change significantly as a result of technical innovation.
          的實際可使用年期可因技術革新而發                            When the actual useful lives of property, plant and equipment are
          生巨大變化。倘因商業及技術環境變                            different from their estimated useful lives due to the change of
          化而令物業、廠房及設備的實際可使                            commercial and technological environment, such difference will
          用年期與其估計可使用年期產生差                             impact the depreciation charges and the amounts of assets written

  租賃 － 估計增量借貸利率                      Leases – Estimating the incremental borrowing rate

  本集團無法輕易釐定租賃內所隱含的                   The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate implicit in a
  利率，因此，使用增量借貸利率（「增                  lease, and therefore, it uses an incremental borrowing rate (“IBR”)
  量借貸利率」）計量租賃負債。增量借                  to measure lease liabilities. The IBR is the rate of interest that
  貸利率為本集團於類似經濟環境中為                   the Group would have to pay to borrow over a similar term, and
  取得與使用權資產價值相近之資產，                   with a similar security, the funds necessary to obtain an asset
  而以類似抵押品與類似期間借入所需                   of a similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic
  資金應支付之利率。因此，增量借貸                   environment. The IBR therefore reflects what the Group”would have
  利率反映了本集團「應支付」的利率，                  to pay”, which requires estimation when no observable rates are
  當無可觀察的利率時（如就並無訂立                   available (such as for subsidiaries that do not enter into financing
  融資交易之附屬公司而言）或當須對                   transactions) or when it needs to be adjusted to reflect the terms
  利率進行調整以反映租賃之條款及條                   and conditions of the lease (for example, when leases are not in
  件時（如當租賃並非以附屬公司之功                   the subsidiary’s functional currency). The Group estimates the
  能貨幣訂立時），則須作出利率估計。                  IBR using observable inputs (such as market interest rates) when
  當可觀察輸入數據可用時，本集團使                   available and is required to make certain entity-specific estimates
  用 可 觀 察 輸 入 數 據（如 市 場 利 率）估        (such as the subsidiary’s stand-alone credit rating).
  算增量借貸利率並須作出若干實體特
  定的估計（如附屬公司的獨立信貸利
  率）。


  燃氣接駁合同的收入確認                        Revenue recognition of gas connection contract

  燃氣接駁合同的建設收入乃使用計量                   Construction revenue from gas connection contracts is recognised
  完全達成服務進度的投入法於一段時                   over time, using an input method to measure progress towards
  間內確認。投入法乃根據所產生成本                   complete satisfaction of the service. The input method recognises
  相對於達成建設服務的估計成本總額                   revenue based on the proportion of the costs incurred relative
  的百分比確認收入。因此，估計合同                   to the estimated total costs for satisfaction of the construction
  成本總額的任何變動可能於該合同期                   services. Accordingly, any changes to the estimated total contract
  限內對各會計期間所確認的合同收入                   cost may have material impact on the contract revenue recognised
  產生重大影響。                            in each accounting period over the contract term.


  存貨撥備                               Allowance for inventories

  本集團根據存貨可變現淨值的估計作                   The Group makes allowance for inventories based on an assessment
  出存貨撥備。倘出現事件或情況變動                   of the net realisable value of inventories. Allowances are applied to
  顯示可變現淨值低於存貨成本，則會                   inventories where events or changes in circumstances indicate that
  對存貨作出撥備。陳舊存貨的確定須                   the net realisable value is lower than the cost of inventories. The
  對存貨狀況及有效性作出判斷及估                    identification of obsolete inventories requires the use of judgment
  計。倘評估存貨的可變現淨值較預期                   and estimates on the conditions and usefulness of the inventories.
  少，或須就存貨確認重大撥備，並將                   In cases where the net realisable value of inventories assessed
  於確認該撥備期間於損益中確認。                    are less than expected, a material recognition of allowance for
                                     inventories may arise, which would be recognised in profit or loss
                                     in the period in which such recognition takes place.

16. 租賃                                       16. LEASES
  本集團作為承租人                                           The Group as a lessee

  本集團有用於其業務營運的樓宇、廠                                   The Group has lease contracts for various items of buildings,
  房、 機 器 及 設 備 多 個 項 目 的 租 賃 合                       plant, machinery and equipment used in its operations. Lump
  約。已提前作出一次性付款以向業主                                   sum payments were generally made upfront to acquire the leased

      41. 資本風險管理                                 41. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT
          本集團管理其資本以維持經營業務所                            The Group manages its capital to maintain a balance between
          得現金流量的持續性及銀行及使用其                            continuity of cash flows from operating activities and the flexibility
          他借貸靈活性之間的平衡。本集團亦                            through the use of bank and other borrowings. The Group also
          定期監控現時及預期流動資金需求及                            monitors the current and expected liquidity requirements and
          符合貸款契諾，以確保具備足夠的營                            its compliance with lending covenants regularly to ensure that
          運資金及充足的融資渠道以滿足流動                            sufficient working capital and adequate committed lines of funding
          資金需求。本集團維持過往年度之整                            are maintained to meet its liquidity requirements. The Group’s
          體策略不變。                                      overall strategy remains unchanged from prior years.


          本集團的資本架構包括負債（包括銀                            The capital structure of the Group consists of debts, which include
          行及其他借貸）及優先票據，不包括                            bank and other borrowings, senior notes, net of bank balances and
          銀行結餘及本公司擁有人應佔股本                             equity attributable to owners of the Company, comprising issued
         （由已發行資本、保留溢利及其他儲備                            capital, retained profits and other reserves.
          組成）。


          本集團管理層定期檢討資本架構。管                            The management of the Group reviews the capital structure on a
          理層認為資本成本及相關風險乃檢討                            regular basis. As part of this review, management considers the
          內容的一部分，並採取適當行動調整                            cost of capital and the associated risks, and takes appropriate
          本集團的資本架構，包括派付股息、                            actions to adjust the Group’s capital structure through the payment
          發行新股、發行新債或償清現有債務。                           of dividends, new share issues as well as the issue of new debts
                                                      or the redemption of existing debts.

        信貸風險                        Credit risk

        倘交易對手未能履行有關各類已              The Group’s maximum exposure to credit risk in the event
        確認金融資產的責任，則本集團              of the counterparties’ failure to perform their obligations in
        的信貸風險將以綜合財務狀況表              relation to each class of recognised financial assets is the
        所列的該等資產賬面值為限。為              carrying amount of those assets as stated in the consolidated
        盡量減低信貸風險，本集團制訂              statement of financial position. In order to minimise the credit
        政策以釐定信貸限額、批核信貸              risk, the Group has policies in place for determination of credit
        額及其他監管程序，以確保能跟              limits, credit approvals and other monitoring procedures to
        進追討有關逾期債務的事宜。               ensure that follow-up action is taken to recover overdue
                                    debts.


        於接納任何新客戶前，本集團就              Before accepting any new customer, the Group carries
        新客戶的信譽進行調查、評估潛              out searches on the creditability of the new customer and
        在客戶的信貸質素並界定客戶的              assesses the potential customer’s credit quality and defines
        信貸限額。賦予客戶的信貸限額              credit limits by customer. Limits attributed to customers are
        每年檢討一次。                     reviewed once a year.


        此外，本集團定期檢討各項個別              In addition, the Group reviews the recoverability of each
        貿易債務是否可收回，以確保就              individual trade debt on a regular basis to ensure that
        不可收回款項作出足夠減值虧               adequate impairment losses are made for irrecoverable
        損。就此，本集團認為信貸風險              amounts. In this regard, the Group considers that the credit
        已大幅減少。                      risk is significantly reduced.


        由於交易對手為具有良好信貸評              The credit risk on bank deposits is limited because the
        級的金融機構，故此銀行存款的              counterparties are financial institutions with high credit
        信貸風險有限。                     standing.

                公平值計量根據公平值計量的輸                                     Fair value measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2, or
                入數據可觀察程度及公平值計量                                     3 based on the degree to which the inputs to the fair value

                詳情如下：                                              described as follows:


                (i)     第一級輸入數據是實體於                                (i)     Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active
                        計量日期可以取得的相同                                        markets for identical assets or liabilities that the entity
                        資產或負債於活躍市場之                                        can access at the measurement date;
                        報價（未經調整）；


                (ii)    第二級輸入數據是就資產                                (ii)    Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices
                        或負債直接或間接地可觀                                        included within Level 1, that are observable for the asset
                        察 之 輸 入 數 據（第 一 級 內                                or liability, either directly or indirectly;
                        包括的報價除外）；及


                (iii)   第三級輸入數據是資產或                                (iii)   Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or
                        負債的不可觀察輸入數據。                                       liability.

      44. 報告期後事件                                 44. EVENTS AFTER THE REPORTING PERIOD
          二 零 二 零 年 伊 始， 新 型 冠 狀 病 毒                  At the beginning of 2020, a novel coronavirus pneumonia
          (COVID-19)感 染 肺 炎（「新 冠 肺 炎」）                (“COVID-19”) swept across the nation. To prevent and control
          肆虐全國，為防控疫情，各地相繼關                            the epidemic, various production and business sites have been
          閉各類生產及經營場所，推遲春節假                            closed down, the resumption of work after the Chinese New
          期復工時間，社會生產和消費出現萎                            Year holiday has been postponed, and social production and
          縮。同時各地政府相繼出台支持中小                            consumption has reduced. At the same time, local governments
          微企業復工復產的扶持措施，以及公                            have successively introduced supportive measures to support
          用事業企業降低收費、延遲繳費等優                            the production resumption of medium, small and micro-sized
          惠政策，這對集團二零二零年業績將                            enterprises, as well as preferential policies for utility companies
          會帶來一定實質影響。                                  to reduce fees and delay payments, which will have a substantial
                                                      impact on the Group’s performance in 2020.
